+   /* #region 🪜️LevelZScale — formula z(k) = k * --z-step (zStep=10), k=0..5 for base..menu (see
+      contract at .🦑️repo/🎫️tickets/26/07/27/UNIFIED-6-LEVEL-UI-SURFACE-SYSTEM/contract.txt). */
+   --z-base: 0;
+   --z-window: 10;

+   --z-navbar: 100;
+   --z-modal: 1000;
+   --z-tutorial: 10000;
+   --glass-saturate: 1.45;

+      pane=2 panel=3 dialog=4 menu=5); 🎨️ui.css and ui/styling/🔣️tokens.json's "levels" block must stay

+      --glass-saturate above (1.45) is reused as-is by the new glass utilities, no duplicate knob. */
+   --level-shade-step: 5%;
+   --glass-alpha-step: 0.12;
+   --glass-blur-step: 0.5rem;
+   --element-shade-step: 6%;
+   --hover-shade-step: 12%;
+   --veil-alpha: 0.4;

+ /* Per-level element foreground — mirrors the --hover-* family. */
+ [data-level="base"] {
+   --border-element-color: var(--element-base);
+ }
+
+ [data-level="window"] {
+   --border-element-color: var(--element-window);
+ }
+
+ [data-level="panel"] {
+   --border-element-color: var(--element-panel);
+ }
+
+ /* #region 🎚️LevelSurfaces — new 6-level surface system (base=0 window=1 pane=2 panel=3 dialog=4
+    menu=5). Each block redeclares the same formula with its literal k baked in, since CSS custom
+    properties cannot be recomputed per-descendant (see contract CSS MECHANISM section); this is
+    still 100% formula-derived off --level-shade-step/--glass-alpha-step/--glass-blur-step/
+    --element-shade-step/--hover-shade-step above, just unrolled six times. base is combined with
+    `:root` so undecorated content defaults to base level. --border-element-color here supersedes
+    the old window/panel-only blocks above and is the only source of it for pane/dialog/menu. */
+ :root,
+ [data-level="base"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(0 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 0 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(0 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(0 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+
+ [data-level="window"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(1 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 1 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(1 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(1 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+
+ [data-level="pane"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(2 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 2 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(2 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(2 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+
+ [data-level="panel"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(3 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 3 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(3 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(3 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+
+ [data-level="dialog"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(4 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 4 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(4 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(4 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+
+ [data-level="menu"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(5 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 5 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(5 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(5 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+ /* #endregion 🎚️LevelSurfaces */
+
+ @utility border-normal {
+   border-color: var(--border-normal-color);
+ }
+
+ @utility border-element {
+   border-color: var(--border-element-color);
+ }

+ @utility divide-element {
+   --tw-divide-color: var(--border-element-color);

+ @utility divide-normal {

+ @property --loading-border-angle {
+   syntax: "<angle>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0deg;
+ }
+
+ @property --loading-border-pulse-opacity {
+   syntax: "<number>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0.55;
+ }
+
+ @property --loading-border-dashoffset {
+   syntax: "<number>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0;
+ }
+
+ /* ⏱️ `@property`-registers the two `animation-duration` tokens below for the same reason the angle above
+    is registered: an unregistered custom property that's only ever read via `var()` inside another
+    hand-authored rule (never through a Tailwind-generated utility class Tailwind's content scan can see)
+    gets silently dropped from `@layer base` by Tailwind v4's production CSS pass, invalidating the
+    `animation`/`animation-duration` declaration that reads it and killing the animation outright — a plain
+    `:root` custom property isn't enough insurance against that pruning; `@property` is. */
+ @property --loading-border-duration {
+   syntax: "<time>";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: 1.6s;
+ }
+
+ @property --waiting-border-angle {
+   syntax: "<angle>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0deg;
+ }
+
+ @property --waiting-border-pulse-opacity {
+   syntax: "<number>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0.7;
+ }
+
+ @property --waiting-border-dashoffset {
+   syntax: "<number>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0;
+ }
+
+ @property --waiting-border-duration {
+   syntax: "<time>";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: 3.2s;
+ }
+
+ /* Same pruning risk as above, but these two are plain static keywords (never theme/appearance-reactive,
+    just like the angle/duration tokens), so `@property` is a complete fix on its own. The six *color*
+    tokens below it (`--border-normal-color` and friends) need a different fix — see the unlayered `:root`
+    re-declaration near the top of this file: their real value is a `var()` chain into theme colors that
+    legitimately differs per light/dark appearance, and `@property`'s `initial-value` must be a single
+    static fallback, so registering them here would have silently locked every border/scrollbar/hover-fill
+    color to one guessed shade regardless of theme — wrong in exactly the way this bug already was. */
+ @property --cursor-help {
+   syntax: "*";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: help;
+ }
+
+ @property --cursor-text {
+   syntax: "*";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: text;
+ }
+
+ @property --introduced-border-duration {
+   syntax: "<time>";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: 1.6s;
+ }
+
+ @property --introduced-border-width {
+   syntax: "<length>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 1px;
+ }
+
+ @property --celebrate-border-angle {
+   syntax: "<angle>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0deg;
+ }
+
+ @property --celebrate-border-duration {
+   syntax: "<time>";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: 1.2s;
+ }
+
+ @property --celebrate-border-padding {
+   syntax: "<length>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 1px;
+ }

+ /* 🎓️ The chrome unit (panel/window/navbar/footer) containing a step's introduce/show target — raised
+    above the single fullscreen introduction veil (z-tutorial) so the real element renders crisp and stays
+    interactive, instead of the veil punching cutout holes around it. !important is required to outrank
+    Panel's inline zIndex; the attribute is unmanaged by React (stamped/cleared imperatively, like
+    data-introduced), so re-renders can't clobber it. */
+ [data-introduction-elevated] {
+   z-index: calc(var(--z-tutorial) + 1) !important;
+ }
+
+ /* 🎓️ Portaled floating UI (Select / Popover / …) leaves the elevated chrome subtree and would otherwise
+    paint under the fullscreen introduction veil (`z-menu` ≪ `z-tutorial`). Raise both the content
+    (so Radix copies the used z-index onto its fixed wrapper) and the Popper wrapper itself. */

+ /* #region 🫨️IconAnim — closed hover micro-animations. Every @keyframes has an identical 0% and 100%
+    frame (shared rest pose), EXCEPT declared full-turn spins (rotate/rotateX/rotateY to ±360deg — a
+    visually identical pose, so the loop is still seamless): rotate-ccw, rotate-cw, loader-2,
+    flip-horizontal, flip-vertical, arrow-right-left, settings, globe. */
+
+ :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]) {
+   transform-origin: 50% 50%;
+ }
+
+ :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]):hover,
+ :where(button, a, summary, [role="button"], [role="menuitem"], [role="menuitemcheckbox"],
+        [role="menuitemradio"], [role="tab"], [role="option"], [role="treeitem"],
+        [role="combobox"]):hover :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]) {
+   animation: var(--icon-animation, none) var(--icon-animation-duration, 500ms)
+              var(--icon-animation-ease, ease-in-out) 1;
+ }
+
+ @media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
+   :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]) {
+     animation: none !important;
+   }
+ }

+ /* folder / folder-open: Lucide draws the tab as a notch in one continuous outline (not a separate closed
+    shape), so there is no literal sub-part to hinge without redrawing the vendored artwork. A folder is
+    rigid, so it must not deform (no skew/squash) — instead it tips as a rigid 3D body around its bottom
+    edge (the same rigid-rotation technique as book-open's page turn), like a laptop lid tipping back. */
+ @keyframes icon-folder-flap {
+   0%,
+   100% {
+     transform: perspective(60px) rotateX(0deg);
+   }
+   45% {
+     transform: perspective(60px) rotateX(-22deg);
+   }
+   75% {
+     transform: perspective(60px) rotateX(4deg);
+   }

+ /* #endregion 🫨️IconAnim */
+
+ /* #region 🔧️IconPartAnim — mechanism-accurate hover animations for icons with cleanly separable SVG
+    parts (verified against committed SVG source). Overrides the whole-icon rigid-body animation
+    above (--icon-animation: none) and instead drives individual svg children by position. Every
+    @keyframes here is closed (0% === 100%). */
+
+ /* Generic per-part engine: any icon can drive its own SVG children via --icon-part-<N>-anim/-origin/-duration.
+    Slots default to none/center/500ms so this is a no-operation for icons that don't set them. */
+ :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]):hover svg > :nth-child(1),
+ :where(button, a, summary, [role="button"], [role="menuitem"], [role="menuitemcheckbox"],
+        [role="menuitemradio"], [role="tab"], [role="option"], [role="treeitem"],
+        [role="combobox"]):hover :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]) svg > :nth-child(1) {
+   transform-box: view-box;
+   transform-origin: var(--icon-part-1-origin, 50% 50%);
+   animation: var(--icon-part-1-anim, none) var(--icon-part-1-duration, 500ms) ease-in-out 1;
+ }
+
+ :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]):hover svg > :nth-child(2),
+ :where(button, a, summary, [role="button"], [role="menuitem"], [role="menuitemcheckbox"],
+        [role="menuitemradio"], [role="tab"], [role="option"], [role="treeitem"],
+        [role="combobox"]):hover :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]) svg > :nth-child(2) {
+   transform-box: view-box;
+   transform-origin: var(--icon-part-2-origin, 50% 50%);
+   animation: var(--icon-part-2-anim, none) var(--icon-part-2-duration, 500ms) ease-in-out 1;
+ }
+
+ :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]):hover svg > :nth-child(3),
+ :where(button, a, summary, [role="button"], [role="menuitem"], [role="menuitemcheckbox"],
+        [role="menuitemradio"], [role="tab"], [role="option"], [role="treeitem"],
+        [role="combobox"]):hover :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]) svg > :nth-child(3) {
+   transform-box: view-box;
+   transform-origin: var(--icon-part-3-origin, 50% 50%);
+   animation: var(--icon-part-3-anim, none) var(--icon-part-3-duration, 500ms) ease-in-out 1;
+ }
+

+ /* #region 🪟️ShellParentHover
+  * Parent-shell edge strokes mirror mode-dock silhouette hover: a dedicated stroke layer
+  * (navbar/footer pseudo-edges, panel/pane `[data-slot="chrome-frame"]`) recolors while the
+  * pointer is inside the parent. Never recolor the parent's own Tailwind `!border-*` — those
+  * utilities win the cascade and made the previous border-*-color :hover rules invisible.
+  * Use :hover only — never :focus-within — so clicking a toggle/pane does not leave the border
+  * stuck emphasized after the cursor leaves.
+  */

+ /* #region 🪟️LevelGlassUtilities — the 3 generic fill utilities that replace ALL bg-base/canvas/
+    window/panel/overlay/temporary surface fills and ALL 5 ui-glass-* classes below, once every
+    consumer switches to `[data-level]` + one of these (see contract). ui-surface is an opaque
+    per-level fill; ui-glass is the per-level glass fill — used identically by a level's body AND
+    its attached chrome (title caps, ribbons, tab bars, rails), since one level must always render
+    as one single appearance, never two; ui-veil is the fullscreen scrim whose host must carry
+    data-level="dialog" for correct tint. In the React layer (ui/js/react/index.tsx), `cn()` extends
+    Tailwind-merge's `bg-color` class group with these three names so composing one alongside a
+    `bg-*` utility (e.g. `bg-transparent`) is a deterministic last-wins conflict, never two classes
+    silently coexisting with the CSS-declaration-order winner picked by accident. */
+ @utility ui-surface {
+   background-color: var(--surface-bg);
+ }
+
+ @utility ui-glass {
+   -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(var(--surface-blur)) saturate(var(--glass-saturate));
+   backdrop-filter: blur(var(--surface-blur)) saturate(var(--glass-saturate));
+   background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--surface-bg) calc(var(--surface-alpha) * 100%), transparent);
+ }
+
+ @utility ui-veil {
+   -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(var(--veil-blur)) saturate(var(--glass-saturate));
+   backdrop-filter: blur(var(--veil-blur)) saturate(var(--glass-saturate));
+   background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--surface-bg) calc(var(--veil-alpha) * 100%), transparent);
+ }
+ /* #endregion 🪟️LevelGlassUtilities */
+
+ /* Shell frame stroke is on [data-slot="chrome-frame"]; inset scroll hosts so scrollbars stay inside. */
+ [data-slot="panel"] [data-slot="scroll-area"],

+ /* #region 🎚️LevelSurfaces — new 6-level surface system (base=0 window=1 pane=2 panel=3 dialog=4
+    menu=5). Each block redeclares the same formula with its literal k baked in, since CSS custom
+    properties cannot be recomputed per-descendant (see contract CSS MECHANISM section); this is
+    still 100% formula-derived off --level-shade-step/--glass-alpha-step/--glass-blur-step/
+    --element-shade-step/--hover-shade-step above, just unrolled six times. base is combined with
+    `:root` so undecorated content defaults to base level. --border-element-color here supersedes
+    the old window/panel-only blocks above and is the only source of it for pane/dialog/menu. */
+ :root,
+ [data-level="base"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(0 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 0 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(0 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(0 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+
+ [data-level="window"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(1 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 1 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(1 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(1 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+
+ [data-level="pane"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(2 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 2 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(2 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(2 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+
+ [data-level="panel"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(3 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 3 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(3 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(3 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+
+ [data-level="dialog"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(4 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 4 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(4 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(4 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+
+ [data-level="menu"] {
+   --surface-bg: color-mix(in oklab, var(--base), var(--foreground) calc(5 * var(--level-shade-step)));
+   --surface-alpha: calc(1 - 5 * var(--glass-alpha-step));
+   --surface-blur: calc(5 * var(--glass-blur-step));
+   --border-element-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--color-gray), var(--foreground) calc(5 * var(--element-shade-step)));
+   --surface-hover: color-mix(in oklab, var(--surface-bg), var(--foreground) var(--hover-shade-step));
+ }
+ /* #endregion 🎚️LevelSurfaces */

+ @property --loading-border-angle {
+   syntax: "<angle>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0deg;
+ }
+
+ @property --loading-border-pulse-opacity {
+   syntax: "<number>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0.55;
+ }
+
+ @property --loading-border-dashoffset {
+   syntax: "<number>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0;
+ }
+
+ /* ⏱️ `@property`-registers the two `animation-duration` tokens below for the same reason the angle above
+    is registered: an unregistered custom property that's only ever read via `var()` inside another
+    hand-authored rule (never through a Tailwind-generated utility class Tailwind's content scan can see)
+    gets silently dropped from `@layer base` by Tailwind v4's production CSS pass, invalidating the
+    `animation`/`animation-duration` declaration that reads it and killing the animation outright — a plain
+    `:root` custom property isn't enough insurance against that pruning; `@property` is. */
+ @property --loading-border-duration {
+   syntax: "<time>";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: 1.6s;
+ }
+
+ @property --waiting-border-angle {
+   syntax: "<angle>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0deg;
+ }
+
+ @property --waiting-border-pulse-opacity {
+   syntax: "<number>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0.7;
+ }
+
+ @property --waiting-border-dashoffset {
+   syntax: "<number>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0;
+ }
+
+ @property --waiting-border-duration {
+   syntax: "<time>";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: 3.2s;
+ }
+
+ /* Same pruning risk as above, but these two are plain static keywords (never theme/appearance-reactive,
+    just like the angle/duration tokens), so `@property` is a complete fix on its own. The six *color*
+    tokens below it (`--border-normal-color` and friends) need a different fix — see the unlayered `:root`
+    re-declaration near the top of this file: their real value is a `var()` chain into theme colors that
+    legitimately differs per light/dark appearance, and `@property`'s `initial-value` must be a single
+    static fallback, so registering them here would have silently locked every border/scrollbar/hover-fill
+    color to one guessed shade regardless of theme — wrong in exactly the way this bug already was. */
+ @property --cursor-help {
+   syntax: "*";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: help;
+ }
+
+ @property --cursor-text {
+   syntax: "*";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: text;
+ }
+
+ @property --introduced-border-duration {
+   syntax: "<time>";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: 1.6s;
+ }
+
+ @property --introduced-border-width {
+   syntax: "<length>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 1px;
+ }
+
+ @property --celebrate-border-angle {
+   syntax: "<angle>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 0deg;
+ }
+
+ @property --celebrate-border-duration {
+   syntax: "<time>";
+   inherits: false;
+   initial-value: 1.2s;
+ }
+
+ @property --celebrate-border-padding {
+   syntax: "<length>";
+   inherits: true;
+   initial-value: 1px;
+ }

+ /* 🎉️ Any element stamped data-celebrated="true" (authored state: "celebrating", or the transient
+    completion stamp from celebrateElements()) rings a spinning conic gradient of the three brand
+    colors whose THICKNESS bursts — the celebratory sibling of the introduced pulse, built on the
+    border-loading masked-ring template. Recolor via --celebrate-border-color-{a,b,c}. */
+ [data-celebrated="true"] {
+   position: relative;
+   --celebrate-conic: conic-gradient(
+     from var(--celebrate-border-angle),
+     var(--celebrate-border-color-a, var(--color-primary)),
+     var(--celebrate-border-color-b, var(--color-secondary)),
+     var(--celebrate-border-color-c, var(--color-tertiary)),
+     var(--celebrate-border-color-a, var(--color-primary))
+   );
+ }
+
+ [data-celebrated="true"]::after {
+   content: "";
+   position: absolute;
+   inset: 0;
+   z-index: 1;
+   pointer-events: none;
+   border-radius: inherit;
+   padding: var(--celebrate-border-padding);
+   background: var(--celebrate-conic);
+   -webkit-mask:
+     linear-gradient(#000 0 0) content-box,
+     linear-gradient(#000 0 0);
+   -webkit-mask-composite: xor;
+   mask:
+     linear-gradient(#000 0 0) content-box,
+     linear-gradient(#000 0 0);
+   mask-composite: exclude;
+ }

+ /* 🪟️ Window silhouettes: the SVG border owns every outer stroke — suppress rectangular body rings. */
+ [data-window-silhouette] [data-slot="window"][data-celebrated="true"]::after,

+   content: none !important;
+   animation: none !important;

+ /* 🎓️ Any element stamped data-introduced="true" (by the introduction system, or declaratively) pulses an
+    inset ring in the highlight color whose THICKNESS breathes — deliberately not the spinning border-loading
+    conic ring. Inset so it stays inside an introduction cutout rather than sitting under the glass veil. */
+ [data-introduced="true"] {

+ /* 🎓️ The chrome unit (panel/window/navbar/footer) containing a step's introduce/show target — raised
+    above the single fullscreen introduction veil (z-tutorial) so the real element renders crisp and stays
+    interactive, instead of the veil punching cutout holes around it. !important is required to outrank
+    Panel's inline zIndex; the attribute is unmanaged by React (stamped/cleared imperatively, like
+    data-introduced), so re-renders can't clobber it. */
+ [data-introduction-elevated] {
+   z-index: calc(var(--z-tutorial) + 1) !important;
+ }
+
+ /* 🎓️ Portaled floating UI (Select / Popover / …) leaves the elevated chrome subtree and would otherwise
+    paint under the fullscreen introduction veil (`z-menu` ≪ `z-tutorial`). Raise both the content
+    (so Radix copies the used z-index onto its fixed wrapper) and the Popper wrapper itself. */
+ [data-introduction-active] [data-slot="select-content"],
+ [data-introduction-active] [data-slot="popover-content"],
+ [data-introduction-active] [data-slot="hover-card-content"],
+ [data-introduction-active] [data-slot="context-menu-content"],
+ [data-introduction-active] [data-radix-popper-content-wrapper] {
+   z-index: calc(var(--z-tutorial) + 3) !important;
+ }

+ /* 🎬️ Real cursor mutes only while a demonstration ghost cursor is actually playing — a separate,
+    playback-scoped attribute from data-introduction-active (which spans the whole tour) so the real

+ .semio-scope[data-introduction-demonstrating] * {
+   cursor: none !important;

+ /* #region 🫨️IconAnim — closed hover micro-animations. Every @keyframes has an identical 0% and 100%
+    frame (shared rest pose), EXCEPT declared full-turn spins (rotate/rotateX/rotateY to ±360deg — a
+    visually identical pose, so the loop is still seamless): rotate-ccw, rotate-cw, loader-2,
+    flip-horizontal, flip-vertical, arrow-right-left, settings, globe. */

+ }

+ /* folder / folder-open: Lucide draws the tab as a notch in one continuous outline (not a separate closed
+    shape), so there is no literal sub-part to hinge without redrawing the vendored artwork. A folder is
+    rigid, so it must not deform (no skew/squash) — instead it tips as a rigid 3D body around its bottom
+    edge (the same rigid-rotation technique as book-open's page turn), like a laptop lid tipping back. */

+ /* #region 🔧️IconPartAnim — mechanism-accurate hover animations for icons with cleanly separable SVG
+    parts (verified against committed SVG source). Overrides the whole-icon rigid-body animation
+    above (--icon-animation: none) and instead drives individual svg children by position. Every
+    @keyframes here is closed (0% === 100%). */
+
+ /* Generic per-part engine: any icon can drive its own SVG children via --icon-part-<N>-anim/-origin/-duration.
+    Slots default to none/center/500ms so this is a no-operation for icons that don't set them. */
+ :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]):hover svg > :nth-child(1),
+ :where(button, a, summary, [role="button"], [role="menuitem"], [role="menuitemcheckbox"],
+        [role="menuitemradio"], [role="tab"], [role="option"], [role="treeitem"],
+        [role="combobox"]):hover :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]) svg > :nth-child(1) {
+   transform-box: view-box;
+   transform-origin: var(--icon-part-1-origin, 50% 50%);
+   animation: var(--icon-part-1-anim, none) var(--icon-part-1-duration, 500ms) ease-in-out 1;
+ }
+
+ :where([data-icon], [data-icon-kind]):hover svg > :nth-child(2),
+ :where(button, a, summary, [role="button"], [role="menuitem"], [role="menuitemcheckbox"],

+ /* #region 🪟️ShellParentHover
+  * Parent-shell edge strokes mirror mode-dock silhouette hover: a dedicated stroke layer
+  * (navbar/footer pseudo-edges, panel/pane `[data-slot="chrome-frame"]`) recolors while the
+  * pointer is inside the parent. Never recolor the parent's own Tailwind `!border-*` — those
+  * utilities win the cascade and made the previous border-*-color :hover rules invisible.
+  * Use :hover only — never :focus-within — so clicking a toggle/pane does not leave the border
+  * stuck emphasized after the cursor leaves.
+  */
+
+ [data-slot="navbar"],
+ [data-slot="footer"] {
+   border-width: 0 !important;
+   border-style: none !important;
+ }
+
+ [data-slot="navbar"]::after,
+ [data-slot="footer"]::before {
+   content: "";
+   position: absolute;
+   left: 0;
+   right: 0;
+   height: var(--stroke-hairline);
+   background-color: var(--border-normal-color);
+   pointer-events: none;
+   transition: background-color 120ms ease;
+   z-index: 50;
+ }
+
+ [data-slot="navbar"]::after {
+   bottom: 0;
+ }
+
+ [data-slot="footer"]::before {
+   top: 0;
+ }
+
+ [data-slot="navbar"]:hover::after,
+ [data-slot="footer"]:hover::before {
+   background-color: var(--border-emphasized-color);
+ }
+
+ [data-slot="navbar"] :is([data-slot="breadcrumb"], [data-slot="button-group"], [data-slot="toggle-group"], [data-slot="action-group"], [data-slot="app-panel-toggle-group"], [data-slot="breadcrumb-item"], [data-slot="breadcrumb-separator-control"]),
+ [data-slot="footer"] :is([data-slot="button-group"], [data-slot="toggle-group"], [data-slot="action-group"]) {
+   border-color: var(--border-normal-color) !important;
+ }
+
+ /* Shell chrome: reinforce level-aware hover on navbar, footer, and toolbar controls. */
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-level]:not([data-state="on"]):not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="footer"] [data-level]:not([data-state="on"]):not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-slot="breadcrumb-link"],
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-slot="breadcrumb-separator-control"] {
+   transition:
+     color 120ms ease,
+     background-color 120ms ease;
+ }
+
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-slot="toggle-group-item"][data-state="on"]:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-slot="button-group-item"][data-state="on"]:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-slot="action-group-item"][data-state="on"]:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-slot="panel-tab-button"][data-state="on"]:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="footer"] [data-slot="toggle-group-item"][data-state="on"]:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="footer"] [data-slot="button-group-item"][data-state="on"]:not(:disabled),

+ [data-slot="footer"] [data-slot="panel-tab-button"][data-state="on"]:not(:disabled),
+ :is([data-slot="toggle-group-item"], [data-slot="button-group-item"], [data-slot="action-group-item"], [data-slot="panel-tab-button"])[data-state="on"]:not(:disabled) {
+   background-color: var(--active-base);
+   border-color: var(--active-base) !important;
+   color: var(--border-emphasized-color);
+ }
+
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-slot="toggle-group-item"][data-state="on"]:hover:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-slot="button-group-item"][data-state="on"]:hover:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-slot="action-group-item"][data-state="on"]:hover:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-slot="panel-tab-button"][data-state="on"]:hover:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="footer"] [data-slot="toggle-group-item"][data-state="on"]:hover:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="footer"] [data-slot="button-group-item"][data-state="on"]:hover:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="footer"] [data-slot="action-group-item"][data-state="on"]:hover:not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="footer"] [data-slot="panel-tab-button"][data-state="on"]:hover:not(:disabled) {
+   background-color: color-mix(in oklab, var(--active-base) 90%, black);
+   color: var(--border-emphasized-color);
+ }
+
+ /* 🪜️ One generic [data-level] selector covers all 6 levels (base/window/pane/panel/dialog/menu) —
+    the per-level bodies were identical no-ops (see contract cleanup step), so no enumeration is
+    needed; this also means new levels (pane/dialog/menu) are covered automatically. */
+ [data-slot="navbar"] [data-level]:not([data-slot$="-group"]):hover:not([data-state="on"]):not(:disabled),
+ [data-slot="footer"] [data-level]:not([data-slot$="-group"]):hover:not([data-state="on"]):not(:disabled),

+ }

+ /* #region 🪟️LevelGlassUtilities — the 3 generic fill utilities that replace ALL bg-base/canvas/
+    window/panel/overlay/temporary surface fills and ALL 5 ui-glass-* classes below, once every
+    consumer switches to `[data-level]` + one of these (see contract). ui-surface is an opaque
+    per-level fill; ui-glass is the per-level glass fill — used identically by a level's body AND
+    its attached chrome (title caps, ribbons, tab bars, rails), since one level must always render
+    as one single appearance, never two; ui-veil is the fullscreen scrim whose host must carry
+    data-level="dialog" for correct tint. In the React layer (ui/js/react/index.tsx), `cn()` extends
+    Tailwind-merge's `bg-color` class group with these three names so composing one alongside a
+    `bg-*` utility (e.g. `bg-transparent`) is a deterministic last-wins conflict, never two classes
+    silently coexisting with the CSS-declaration-order winner picked by accident. */
+ @utility ui-surface {
+   background-color: var(--surface-bg);
+ }
+
+ @utility ui-glass {
+   -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(var(--surface-blur)) saturate(var(--glass-saturate));
+   backdrop-filter: blur(var(--surface-blur)) saturate(var(--glass-saturate));
+   background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--surface-bg) calc(var(--surface-alpha) * 100%), transparent);
+ }
+
+ @utility ui-veil {
+   -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(var(--veil-blur)) saturate(var(--glass-saturate));
+   backdrop-filter: blur(var(--veil-blur)) saturate(var(--glass-saturate));
+   background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--surface-bg) calc(var(--veil-alpha) * 100%), transparent);
+ }
+ /* #endregion 🪟️LevelGlassUtilities */
+
+ /* Shell frame stroke is on [data-slot="chrome-frame"]; inset scroll hosts so scrollbars stay inside. */
+ [data-slot="panel"] [data-slot="scroll-area"],
+ [data-slot="pane"] [data-slot="scroll-area"] {
+   box-sizing: border-box;
+   padding: var(--stroke-hairline);
+ }
+
+ [data-slot="chrome-frame"] {
+   border-width: var(--stroke-hairline);
+   border-style: solid;
+   border-color: var(--border-normal-color) !important;
+   transition: border-color 120ms ease;
+ }
+
+ :is([data-slot="panel"][data-panel="mobilePanel"]):hover [data-slot="chrome-frame"] {
+   border-color: var(--border-emphasized-color) !important;
+ }

+ /* 🎓️ Introduction step body: emphasize only the paragraph under the pointer — never sibling
+  * paragraphs, footer step-count chip, or checklist. Title chip + drag handle emphasize via
+  * parent info-box hover (see rules below). */
+ [data-slot="introduction-info-box"] [data-slot="introduction-body-paragraph"]:hover {
+   color: var(--border-emphasized-color);
+   transition: color 120ms ease;
+ }

+ /* 🪟️ U-gap must punch through — never inherit cap/body glass or a stack rectangle fill. */

+   background: transparent !important;
+   background-color: transparent !important;
+   -webkit-backdrop-filter: none !important;
+   backdrop-filter: none !important;
+   box-shadow: none !important;
+ }

+ /* 🪟️ Docked chips: no piecewise outer borders or inset separators — the silhouette SVG owns
+  * every stroke. The immediate flowing control also drops its generic control-group frame while
+  * deliberate dividers nested inside multi-tab/multi-button groups remain intact. */
+ [data-window-silhouette-chip],
+ [data-window-silhouette-chip] > * {
+   border-width: 0 !important;
+   border-style: none !important;
+   box-shadow: none;

+ /* #region 🫳️DragHandleHover
+  * Grip paints its own muted color at rest, so it cannot inherit the parent
+  * `hover:text-emphasized`. Mirror hover-scope emphasis onto the grip whenever
+  * the pointer is inside that scope — same as the label/icon beside it.
+  * Hover-scopes keep nested rows as siblings (not descendants), so this does
+  * not light up parent grips while hovering a child row.
+  */
+ [data-hover-scope]:hover [data-slot="drag-handle"] {
+   color: var(--border-emphasized-color);
+ }
